===== ./.github/workflows/build.yml =====
name: Build & Release APK
on:
  push:
    branches: [ main ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Set up JDK 17
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '17'

      - name: Setup Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v3
        with:
          gradle-version: 8.9

      - name: Setup Android NDK r25c
        uses: nttld/setup-ndk@v1
        with:
          ndk-version: r25c

      - name: Build hev-socks5-tunnel
        run: |
          curl -L -o hev.tar.xz https://github.com/heiher/hev-socks5-tunnel/releases/download/2.14.4/hev-socks5-tunnel-2.14.4.tar.xz
          tar -xf hev.tar.xz
          mv hev-socks5-tunnel-2.14.4 hev-src
          cd hev-src
          sed -i 's/LOCAL_CFLAGS :=/LOCAL_CFLAGS := -DPKGNAME=com\/mdkdw1\/myinternet -DCLSNAME=HevTunnelManager/' Android.mk
          $ANDROID_NDK_HOME/ndk-build \
            NDK_PROJECT_PATH=. \
            APP_BUILD_SCRIPT=Android.mk \
            APP_ABI=arm64-v8a \
            APP_PLATFORM=android-24 \
            APP_ALLOW_MISSING_DEPS=true \
            NDK_LIBS_OUT=./libs \
            NDK_OUT=./obj
          cd ..
          mkdir -p app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a
          cp hev-src/libs/arm64-v8a/*.so app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a/ || true
          ls -la app/src/main/jniLibs/arm64-v8a/ || true

      - name: Bump version from latest tag
        id: bump
        run: |
          git fetch --tags
          LAST=$(git tag -l 'v*' | sort -V | tail -1 | sed 's/^v//')
          LAST=${LAST:-0.000}
          NEW_NAME=$(python3 -c "print(f'{${LAST} + 0.001:.3f}')")
          NEW_CODE=$(( $(git rev-list --count HEAD) ))
          echo "new_name=$NEW_NAME" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "new_code=$NEW_CODE" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT

      - name: Build Debug APK
        run: |
          gradle assembleDebug --no-daemon --stacktrace \
            -PversionName=${{ steps.bump.outputs.new_name }} \
            -PversionCode=${{ steps.bump.outputs.new_code }}

      - name: Verify APK exists
        run: |
          find app/build/outputs -name "*.apk" -ls
          test -f app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk

      - name: Upload Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: myinternet-apk
          path: app/build/outputs/apk/debug/*.apk
          retention-days: 90

      - name: Create Release with gh
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
        run: |
          TAG="v${{ steps.bump.outputs.new_name }}"
          gh release view "$TAG" >/dev/null 2>&1 || gh release create "$TAG" --title "MyInternet $TAG" --generate-notes
          gh release upload "$TAG" app/build/outputs/apk/debug/*.apk --clobber
-e 

===== ./bump_version.py =====
import re

with open("version.properties", "r") as f:
    content = f.read()

ver_name = float(re.search(r"VERSION_NAME=([\d.]+)", content).group(1))
ver_code = int(re.search(r"VERSION_CODE=(\d+)", content).group(1))

new_name = round(ver_name + 0.001, 3)
new_code = ver_code + 1

with open("version.properties", "w") as f:
    f.write(f"VERSION_NAME={new_name:.3f}\nVERSION_CODE={new_code}\n")

print(f"Bumped: {ver_name} -> {new_name:.3f} (code {ver_code} -> {new_code})")
-e 

===== ./README.md =====
# myinternet

Termux/Kotlin 기반 테더링 프록시 앱.

## 빌드
GitHub에 push 하면 Actions가 자동으로 APK를 빌드하고 Release에 올립니다.
버전은 `version.properties` 기준으로 push마다 0.001씩 증가합니다.
-e 

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/MainActivity.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.Manifest
import android.app.AlertDialog
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.net.Uri
import android.net.VpnService
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.text.InputType
import android.view.View
import android.widget.*
import androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatActivity
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import com.journeyapps.barcodescanner.ScanContract
import com.journeyapps.barcodescanner.ScanOptions

class MainActivity : AppCompatActivity() {
    private lateinit var settings: SettingsManager
    private lateinit var serverPanel: LinearLayout
    private lateinit var clientPanel: LinearLayout

    private lateinit var statusText: TextView
    private lateinit var statsText: TextView
    private lateinit var toggleBtn: Button
    private lateinit var hotspotInfoText: TextView
    private lateinit var qrImage: ImageView

    private lateinit var vpnStatusText: TextView
    private lateinit var vpnInfoText: TextView
    private lateinit var hostInput: EditText
    private lateinit var portInput: EditText
    private lateinit var userInput: EditText
    private lateinit var passInput: EditText
    private lateinit var clientStatusText: TextView

    private var localHotspot: LocalHotspotManager? = null
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val VPN_REQ = 200
    private val PERM_REQ = 300

    private val versionName: String by lazy {
        try { packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, 0).versionName ?: "0.000" }
        catch (_: Exception) { "0.000" }
    }

    private val scanLauncher = registerForActivityResult(ScanContract()) { result ->
        val text = result.contents ?: return@registerForActivityResult
        handleScannedText(text)
    }

    private val refresh = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            val srv = Socks5Service.server
            if (srv != null && Socks5Service.isRunning)
                statsText.text = "활성 ${srv.activeConnections.get()} · 총 ${srv.totalConnections.get()}"
            else statsText.text = ""

            if (MyVpnService.isRunning) {
                vpnStatusText.text = "🟢 VPN 연결됨"
                vpnInfoText.text = "서버: ${settings.srvHost}:${settings.srvPort}"
            } else {
                vpnStatusText.text = "⚪ VPN 중지"
                vpnInfoText.text = MyVpnService.lastError?.let { "오류: $it" } ?: ""
            }
            handler.postDelayed(this, 1000)
        }
    }

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        CrashHandler.init(this)
        settings = SettingsManager(this)
        localHotspot = LocalHotspotManager(this)

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33 &&
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED)
            ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, arrayOf(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS), 100)

        buildUI()
        handler.post(refresh)
        ensurePermissions()
    }

    private fun ensurePermissions() {
        val perms = mutableListOf<String>()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.NEARBY_WIFI_DEVICES) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED)
                perms.add(Manifest.permission.NEARBY_WIFI_DEVICES)
        } else {
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED)
                perms.add(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION)
        }
        if (perms.isNotEmpty()) ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, perms.toTypedArray(), PERM_REQ)
    }

    private fun buildUI() {
        val scroll = ScrollView(this)
        val root = LinearLayout(this).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL; setPadding(48, 64, 48, 48)
        }
        scroll.addView(root)
        root.addView(TextView(this).apply { text = "🌐 MyInternet"; textSize = 26f })
        root.addView(TextView(this).apply { text = "v$versionName"; textSize = 13f; setTextColor(0xFF888888.toInt()); setPadding(0, 0, 0, 20) })

        val modeGroup = RadioGroup(this).apply { orientation = RadioGroup.HORIZONTAL; setPadding(0, 0, 0, 16) }
        val rbServer = RadioButton(this).apply { text = "서버 (공유 폰)"; id = View.generateViewId(); isChecked = true }
        val rbClient = RadioButton(this).apply { text = "클라이언트"; id = View.generateViewId() }
        modeGroup.addView(rbServer); modeGroup.addView(rbClient)
        modeGroup.setOnCheckedChangeListener { _, id ->
            val isServer = id == rbServer.id
            serverPanel.visibility = if (isServer) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            clientPanel.visibility = if (isServer) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
        }
        root.addView(modeGroup)

        serverPanel = buildServerPanel()
        clientPanel = buildClientPanel().apply { visibility = View.GONE }
        root.addView(serverPanel); root.addView(clientPanel)

        root.addView(title("업데이트"))
        root.addView(Button(this).apply { text = "업데이트 확인"; setOnClickListener { checkUpdate() } })

        root.addView(title("디버그"))
        root.addView(Button(this).apply {
            text = "📜 앱 로그 보기 (크래시 원인 확인)"
            setOnClickListener {
                startActivity(Intent(this@MainActivity, LogcatActivity::class.java))
            }
        })

        setContentView(scroll)
    }

    private fun buildServerPanel(): LinearLayout {
        val p = LinearLayout(this).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL }
        
        p.addView(title("1️⃣ 원터치 서버 시작 (프록시 + 핫스팟)"))
        toggleBtn = Button(this).apply {
            text = if (Socks5Service.isRunning) "■ 서버 중지" else "▶ 서버 시작 (한 번에)"
            setOnClickListener { 
                if (Socks5Service.isRunning) stopServer() else startServerAndHotspot() 
            }
        }
        p.addView(toggleBtn)
        statusText = TextView(this).apply { text = if (Socks5Service.isRunning) "🟢 실행 중" else "⚪ 중지됨"; textSize = 16f; setPadding(0, 16, 0, 8) }
        p.addView(statusText)
        statsText = TextView(this).apply { textSize = 13f; setTextColor(0xFF666666.toInt()) }
        p.addView(statsText)

        p.addView(title("2️⃣ 클라이언트 접속 정보 (QR)"))
        hotspotInfoText = TextView(this).apply { textSize = 14f; setPadding(0, 8, 0, 8); typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE }
        p.addView(hotspotInfoText)
        qrImage = ImageView(this).apply { layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(500, 500) }
        p.addView(qrImage)

        return p
    }

    private fun startServerAndHotspot() {
        if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.NEARBY_WIFI_DEVICES) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED &&
            Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            ensurePermissions()
            toast("권한을 허용한 후 다시 눌러주세요")
            return
        }
        
        startServer()
        
        localHotspot?.startHotspot(object : LocalHotspotManager.HotspotCallback {
            override fun onHotspotStarted(ssid: String, password: String) {
                handler.postDelayed({
                    val myIp = getServerHotspotIp()
                    hotspotInfoText.text = "SSID: $ssid\nPW: $password\n내 IP: $myIp"
                    val cfg = "HOTSPOT:$ssid:$password:$myIp"
                    qrImage.setImageBitmap(QrHelper.generate(cfg))
                }, 1000)
            }
            override fun onHotspotFailed(reason: String) {
                hotspotInfoText.text = "핫스팟 실패: $reason"
            }
            override fun onClientConnected() {}
            override fun onClientDisconnected() {}
        })
    }

    private fun buildClientPanel(): LinearLayout {
        val p = LinearLayout(this).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL }

        p.addView(title("📷 QR 스캔으로 핫스팟 연결"))
        p.addView(Button(this).apply {
            text = "📷 서버 QR 스캔"
            setOnClickListener {
                val opts = ScanOptions().apply {
                    setDesiredBarcodeFormats(ScanOptions.QR_CODE)
                    setPrompt("서버 화면의 QR을 스캔하세요")
                    setBeepEnabled(false)
                }
                scanLauncher.launch(opts)
            }
        })
        clientStatusText = TextView(this).apply { textSize = 13f; setPadding(0, 8, 0, 8) }
        p.addView(clientStatusText)

        p.addView(title("수동 설정 (VPN)"))
        hostInput = EditText(this).apply { hint = "서버 IP"; setText(settings.srvHost) }
        portInput = EditText(this).apply { hint = "포트"; setText(settings.srvPort.toString()); inputType = InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER }
        userInput = EditText(this).apply { hint = "사용자명"; setText(settings.srvUser) }
        passInput = EditText(this).apply { hint = "비밀번호"; setText(settings.srvPass); inputType = InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD }
        listOf(hostInput, portInput, userInput, passInput).forEach { p.addView(it) }
        
        p.addView(Button(this).apply {
            text = "VPN 연결"
            setOnClickListener {
                saveClientFields()
                if (MyVpnService.isRunning) stopVpn() else requestVpn()
            }
        })
        vpnStatusText = TextView(this).apply { text = "⚪ VPN 중지"; textSize = 16f; setPadding(0, 16, 0, 8) }
        p.addView(vpnStatusText)
        vpnInfoText = TextView(this).apply { textSize = 13f; setTextColor(0xFF666666.toInt()) }
        p.addView(vpnInfoText)

        return p
    }

    private fun handleScannedText(text: String) {
        if (text.startsWith("HOTSPOT:")) {
            val parts = text.removePrefix("HOTSPOT:").split(":")
            if (parts.size >= 2) {
                val ssid = parts[0]
                val pw = parts[1]
                val qrIp = parts.getOrNull(2)
                clientStatusText.text = "핫스팟 연결 중: $ssid"
                localHotspot?.connectToHotspot(ssid, pw, object : LocalHotspotManager.HotspotCallback {
                    override fun onHotspotStarted(ssid: String, password: String) {}
                    override fun onHotspotFailed(reason: String) { clientStatusText.text = "연결 실패: $reason" }
                    override fun onClientConnected() {
                        val serverIp = qrIp ?: getGatewayIp() ?: "192.168.43.1"
                        clientStatusText.text = "✅ 핫스팟 연결됨. 서버 IP: $serverIp"
                        applyClientConfig(serverIp, 1080, "myinternet", "myinternet")
                        handler.postDelayed({ requestVpn() }, 1000)
                    }
                    override fun onClientDisconnected() { clientStatusText.text = "연결 끊김" }
                })
            }
        } else {
            val parts = text.trim().split(":")
            if (parts.size < 2) return
            applyClientConfig(parts[0], parts.getOrNull(1)?.toIntOrNull() ?: 1080, parts.getOrNull(2) ?: "myinternet", parts.getOrNull(3) ?: "myinternet")
            toast("IP 설정 완료. VPN 연결...")
            handler.postDelayed({ requestVpn() }, 500)
        }
    }

    private fun getServerHotspotIp(): String {
        try {
            val interfaces = java.net.NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces().toList()
            for (intf in interfaces) {
                if (intf.isUp && !intf.isLoopback) {
                    val name = intf.name
                    if (name.startsWith("ap") || name.startsWith("swlan") || name.contains("wlan1") || name.contains("softap")) {
                        for (addr in intf.inetAddresses.toList()) {
                            if (addr is java.net.Inet4Address && !addr.isLoopbackAddress) {
                                val ip = addr.hostAddress
                                if (ip != null && (ip.startsWith("192.168.") || ip.startsWith("10."))) return ip
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            for (intf in interfaces) {
                for (addr in intf.inetAddresses.toList()) {
                    if (addr is java.net.Inet4Address && !addr.isLoopbackAddress) {
                        val ip = addr.hostAddress
                        if (ip != null && ip.startsWith("10.204.")) return ip
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        return "192.168.43.1"
    }

    private fun getGatewayIp(): String? {
        try {
            val wm = applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as android.net.wifi.WifiManager
            val ip = wm.dhcpInfo.gateway
            if (ip != 0) return "${ip and 0xff}.${(ip shr 8) and 0xff}.${(ip shr 16) and 0xff}.${(ip shr 24) and 0xff}"
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        return null
    }

    private fun applyClientConfig(host: String, port: Int, user: String, pass: String) {
        hostInput.setText(host); portInput.setText(port.toString()); userInput.setText(user); passInput.setText(pass)
        settings.srvHost = host; settings.srvPort = port; settings.srvUser = user; settings.srvPass = pass
    }

    private fun saveClientFields() {
        settings.srvHost = hostInput.text.toString().trim()
        settings.srvPort = portInput.text.toString().toIntOrNull() ?: 1080
        settings.srvUser = userInput.text.toString().ifBlank { "myinternet" }
        settings.srvPass = passInput.text.toString().ifBlank { "myinternet" }
    }

    private fun requestVpn() {
        if (settings.srvHost.isBlank()) { toast("서버 정보 없음"); return }
        val intent = VpnService.prepare(this)
        if (intent != null) startActivityForResult(intent, VPN_REQ) else startVpnService()
    }
    private fun startVpnService() {
        val i = Intent(this, MyVpnService::class.java).apply { action = MyVpnService.ACTION_START }
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) startForegroundService(i) else startService(i)
        toast("VPN 시작")
    }
    private fun stopVpn() {
        startService(Intent(this, MyVpnService::class.java).apply { action = MyVpnService.ACTION_STOP })
        toast("VPN 해제")
    }
    @Deprecated("Deprecated in API 30")
    override fun onActivityResult(requestCode: Int, resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data)
        if (requestCode == VPN_REQ) { if (resultCode == RESULT_OK) startVpnService() else toast("VPN 권한 필요") }
    }
    private fun startServer() {
        val i = Intent(this, Socks5Service::class.java).apply { action = Socks5Service.ACTION_START }
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) startForegroundService(i) else startService(i)
        toggleBtn.text = "■ 서버 중지"; statusText.text = "🟢 실행 중"; toast("서버 시작")
    }
    private fun stopServer() {
        startService(Intent(this, Socks5Service::class.java).apply { action = Socks5Service.ACTION_STOP })
        localHotspot?.stopHotspot()
        toggleBtn.text = "▶ 서버 시작 (한 번에)"; statusText.text = "⚪ 중지됨"; hotspotInfoText.text = "중지됨"
        toast("서버 및 핫스팟 중지")
    }
    private fun checkUpdate() {
        toast("확인 중...")
        UpdateChecker().check(versionName) { info ->
            if (info != null) AlertDialog.Builder(this)
                .setTitle("업데이트 발견").setMessage("v${info.version} (현재 v$versionName)")
                .setPositiveButton("다운로드") { _, _ -> startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(info.url))) }
                .setNegativeButton("나중에", null).show()
            else toast("최신 버전 (v$versionName)")
        }
    }
    private fun title(s: String) = TextView(this).apply { text = s; textSize = 17f; setPadding(0, 32, 0, 8); setTypeface(typeface, Typeface.BOLD) }
    private fun toast(m: String) = Toast.makeText(this, m, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()

    override fun onDestroy() { super.onDestroy(); handler.removeCallbacks(refresh); localHotspot?.stopHotspot() }
}
-e 

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/Socks5Server.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import java.io.DataInputStream
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.net.*
import java.util.concurrent.*
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.*
import kotlin.concurrent.thread

class Socks5Server(
    private val port: Int,
    private val username: String,
    private val password: String
) {
    @Volatile private var running = false
    private var serverSocket: ServerSocket? = null
    private val activeSockets = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet<Socket>()
    private val udpSessions = ConcurrentHashMap<String, UdpRelaySession>()

    val activeConnections = AtomicInteger(0)
    val totalConnections = AtomicInteger(0)
    val bytesTransferred = AtomicLong(0)

    fun start() {
        if (running) return
        running = true
        thread(name = "socks5-accept") {
            try {
                serverSocket = ServerSocket().apply {
                    reuseAddress = true
                    bind(InetSocketAddress(port), 50)   // 모든 인터페이스(로컬 핫스팟 포함)
                }
                while (running) {
                    val c = try { serverSocket?.accept() ?: break } catch (_: Exception) { break }
                    thread { handle(c) }
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        running = false
        try { serverSocket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        serverSocket = null
        activeSockets.forEach { try { it.close() } catch (_: Exception) {} }
        activeSockets.clear()
        udpSessions.values.forEach { it.close() }
        udpSessions.clear()
    }

    private fun handle(s: Socket) {
        activeSockets.add(s); totalConnections.incrementAndGet()
        var authenticated = false
        try {
            s.tcpNoDelay = true
            s.soTimeout = 15000                       // 핸드셰이크 구간만 타임아웃
            val i = DataInputStream(s.getInputStream()); val o = s.getOutputStream()

            if (i.readUnsignedByte() != 0x05) return
            val n = i.readUnsignedByte(); val m = ByteArray(n); i.readFully(m)
            if (!m.contains(0x02.toByte())) { o.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0xFF.toByte())); return }
            o.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x02)); o.flush()

            if (i.readUnsignedByte() != 0x01) return
            val un = ByteArray(i.readUnsignedByte()); i.readFully(un)
            val pw = ByteArray(i.readUnsignedByte()); i.readFully(pw)
            if (String(un) != username || String(pw) != password) {
                o.write(byteArrayOf(0x01, 0x01)); return
            }
            o.write(byteArrayOf(0x01, 0x00)); o.flush()
            authenticated = true; activeConnections.incrementAndGet()

            if (i.readUnsignedByte() != 0x05) return
            val cmd = i.readUnsignedByte(); i.readUnsignedByte()
            val host: String
            when (i.readUnsignedByte()) {
                0x01 -> { val b = ByteArray(4); i.readFully(b)
                    host = InetAddress.getByAddress(b).hostAddress ?: return }
                0x03 -> { val b = ByteArray(i.readUnsignedByte()); i.readFully(b)
                    host = String(b) }
                0x04 -> { val b = ByteArray(16); i.readFully(b)
                    host = InetAddress.getByAddress(b).hostAddress ?: return }
                else -> return
            }
            val dstPort = i.readUnsignedShort()
            s.soTimeout = 0

            when (cmd) {
                0x01 -> handleConnect(s, o, host, dstPort)
                0x03 -> handleUdpAssociate(s, o)
                else -> o.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x07, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0))
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        } finally {
            try { s.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            activeSockets.remove(s)
            if (authenticated) activeConnections.decrementAndGet()
        }
    }

    private fun handleConnect(client: Socket, out: OutputStream, host: String, port: Int) {
        val remote = Socket()
        try {
            remote.tcpNoDelay = true                  // 게임 소패킷 지연 방지
            remote.connect(InetSocketAddress(host, port), 10000)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            try { remote.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            out.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x05, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)); return
        }
        activeSockets.add(remote)
        try {
            out.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0)); out.flush()
            val t = thread { pipe(client, remote) }
            pipe(remote, client); t.join()
        } finally {
            activeSockets.remove(remote)
            try { remote.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    private fun handleUdpAssociate(client: Socket, out: OutputStream) {
        val relay = DatagramSocket(0, InetAddress.getByName("0.0.0.0"))
        relay.soTimeout = 0
        val bndPort = relay.localPort
        // BND.ADDR = 클라이언트가 접속한 서버 IP (0.0.0.0 을 주면 클라이언트가 자기 자신으로 보내버림)
        val bnd = client.localAddress.address.takeIf { it.size == 4 } ?: ByteArray(4)
        out.write(byteArrayOf(0x05, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01) + bnd +
            byteArrayOf((bndPort ushr 8).toByte(), bndPort.toByte())); out.flush()

        val session = UdpRelaySession(relay, client)
        val key = "${client.inetAddress.hostAddress}:${client.port}"
        udpSessions[key] = session
        session.start()

        try {
            val buf = ByteArray(1)
            while (running && client.getInputStream().read(buf) != -1) { /* 제어 연결 유지 */ }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        session.close()
        udpSessions.remove(key)
    }

    private class UdpRelaySession(
        private val relay: DatagramSocket,
        private val client: Socket
    ) {
        @Volatile private var running = true
        private val clientAddr: InetAddress = client.inetAddress
        @Volatile private var clientUdpPort = 0
        private val upstream = DatagramSocket()
        private val resolved = ConcurrentHashMap<String, InetAddress>()

        init {
            try {
                upstream.receiveBufferSize = 262144; upstream.sendBufferSize = 262144
                relay.receiveBufferSize = 262144; relay.sendBufferSize = 262144
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        fun start() {
            thread(name = "udp-client-${relay.localPort}") { clientLoop() }
            thread(name = "udp-upstream-${relay.localPort}") { upstreamLoop() }
        }

        fun close() {
            running = false
            try { relay.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            try { upstream.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        // 클라이언트 → 인터넷
        private fun clientLoop() {
            val buf = ByteArray(65535)
            val p = DatagramPacket(buf, buf.size)
            while (running) {
                try {
                    p.length = buf.size
                    relay.receive(p)
                    if (p.address != clientAddr) continue
                    clientUdpPort = p.port
                    val (dst, dstPort, off) = parse(buf, p.length) ?: continue
                    upstream.send(DatagramPacket(buf, off, p.length - off, dst, dstPort))
                } catch (_: Exception) { if (!running) break }
            }
        }

        // 인터넷 → 클라이언트 (상시 수신)
        private fun upstreamLoop() {
            val buf = ByteArray(65535)
            val p = DatagramPacket(buf, buf.size)
            while (running) {
                try {
                    p.length = buf.size
                    upstream.receive(p)
                    val port = clientUdpPort
                    if (port == 0) continue
                    val header = buildUdpHeader(p.address, p.port)
                    val out = ByteArray(header.size + p.length)
                    System.arraycopy(header, 0, out, 0, header.size)
                    System.arraycopy(buf, 0, out, header.size, p.length)
                    relay.send(DatagramPacket(out, out.size, clientAddr, port))
                } catch (_: Exception) { if (!running) break }
            }
        }

        // 반환: (목적지 주소, 포트, 페이로드 시작 오프셋)
        private fun parse(d: ByteArray, len: Int): Triple<InetAddress, Int, Int>? {
            if (len < 10) return null
            val addr: InetAddress
            val off: Int
            when (d[3].toInt() and 0xFF) {
                0x01 -> { addr = InetAddress.getByAddress(d.copyOfRange(4, 8)); off = 8 }
                0x03 -> {
                    val l = d[4].toInt() and 0xFF
                    if (len < 5 + l + 2) return null
                    val name = String(d, 5, l)
                    addr = resolved.getOrPut(name) { InetAddress.getByName(name) }
                    off = 5 + l
                }
                0x04 -> {
                    if (len < 22) return null
                    addr = InetAddress.getByAddress(d.copyOfRange(4, 20)); off = 20
                }
                else -> return null
            }
            if (len < off + 2) return null
            val port = ((d[off].toInt() and 0xFF) shl 8) or (d[off + 1].toInt() and 0xFF)
            return Triple(addr, port, off + 2)
        }

        private fun buildUdpHeader(addr: InetAddress, port: Int): ByteArray {
            val ip = addr.address
            val h = ByteArray(4 + ip.size + 2)
            h[3] = (if (ip.size == 4) 0x01 else 0x04).toByte()
            System.arraycopy(ip, 0, h, 4, ip.size)
            h[4 + ip.size] = (port ushr 8).toByte()
            h[5 + ip.size] = port.toByte()
            return h
        }
    }

    private fun pipe(from: Socket, to: Socket) {
        try {
            val a = from.getInputStream(); val b = to.getOutputStream()
            val buf = ByteArray(16384)
            while (running) {
                val r = a.read(buf); if (r < 0) break
                b.write(buf, 0, r); b.flush()
                bytesTransferred.addAndGet(r.toLong())
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        try { to.shutdownOutput() } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }
}

package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences

class SettingsManager(context: Context) {
    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("myinternet", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    // 서버 모드 설정
    var port: Int
        get() = prefs.getInt("port", 1080)
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putInt("port", v).apply()
    var username: String
        get() = prefs.getString("username", "myinternet") ?: "myinternet"
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("username", v).apply()
    var password: String
        get() = prefs.getString("password", "myinternet") ?: "myinternet"
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("password", v).apply()

    // 클라이언트 모드 설정
    var srvHost: String
        get() = prefs.getString("srvHost", "") ?: ""
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("srvHost", v).apply()
    var srvPort: Int
        get() = prefs.getInt("srvPort", 1080)
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putInt("srvPort", v).apply()
    var srvUser: String
        get() = prefs.getString("srvUser", "myinternet") ?: "myinternet"
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("srvUser", v).apply()
    var srvPass: String
        get() = prefs.getString("srvPass", "myinternet") ?: "myinternet"
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("srvPass", v).apply()

    // UI 모드 (server / client)
    var lastMode: String
        get() = prefs.getString("lastMode", "server") ?: "server"
        set(v) = prefs.edit().putString("lastMode", v).apply()
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/Socks5Service.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.app.*
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder

class Socks5Service : Service() {
    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "socks5_service"
        const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1001
        const val ACTION_START = "START"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "STOP"

        @Volatile var isRunning = false
        @Volatile var server: Socks5Server? = null
    }

    private var discovery: DiscoveryServer? = null

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val ch = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "MyInternet", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(ch)
        }
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_START -> startServer()
            ACTION_STOP -> { stopServer(); stopSelf() }
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startServer() {
        if (isRunning) return
        val s = SettingsManager(this)
        startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification("SOCKS5 서버 · 포트 ${s.port} · 자동 발견 켜짐"))
        server = Socks5Server(s.port, s.username, s.password).also { it.start() }
        discovery = DiscoveryServer(s.port, s.username, s.password).also { it.start() }
        isRunning = true
    }

    private fun stopServer() {
        discovery?.stop(); discovery = null
        server?.stop(); server = null
        isRunning = false
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") stopForeground(true)
    }

    override fun onDestroy() { stopServer(); super.onDestroy() }

    private fun buildNotification(text: String): Notification {
        val stopIntent = Intent(this, Socks5Service::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_STOP }
        val pi = PendingIntent.getService(this, 0, stopIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
        val b = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
            Notification.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID)
        else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") Notification.Builder(this)
        b.setContentTitle("MyInternet").setContentText(text)
            .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.stat_sys_upload)
            .setOngoing(true)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
            b.addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(null, "중지", pi).build())
        else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") b.addAction(android.R.drawable.ic_delete, "중지", pi)
        return b.build()
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/UpdateChecker.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL

class UpdateChecker {
    data class ReleaseInfo(val version: String, val url: String)

    fun check(currentVersion: String, callback: (ReleaseInfo?) -> Unit) {
        Thread {
            try {
                val conn = URL("https://api.github.com/repos/mdkdw1-ui/myinternet/releases/latest")
                    .openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
                conn.setRequestProperty("Accept", "application/vnd.github+json")
                conn.connectTimeout = 5000
                conn.readTimeout = 5000
                if (conn.responseCode != 200) { post(callback, null); return@Thread }
                val json = JSONObject(conn.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText())
                val tag = json.getString("tag_name").removePrefix("v")
                val assets = json.getJSONArray("assets")
                var apk = ""
                for (k in 0 until assets.length()) {
                    val a = assets.getJSONObject(k)
                    if (a.getString("name").endsWith(".apk")) { apk = a.getString("browser_download_url"); break }
                }
                post(callback, if (isNewer(tag, currentVersion)) ReleaseInfo(tag, apk) else null)
            } catch (_: Exception) { post(callback, null) }
        }.start()
    }

    private fun post(cb: (ReleaseInfo?) -> Unit, r: ReleaseInfo?) {
        Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { cb(r) }
    }

    private fun isNewer(remote: String, current: String): Boolean {
        val a = remote.split(".").mapNotNull { it.toIntOrNull() }
        val b = current.split(".").mapNotNull { it.toIntOrNull() }
        for (i in 0 until maxOf(a.size, b.size)) {
            val x = a.getOrElse(i) { 0 }; val y = b.getOrElse(i) { 0 }
            if (x > y) return true
            if (x < y) return false
        }
        return false
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/MyVpnService.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.app.*
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.VpnService
import android.os.Build
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream

class MyVpnService : VpnService() {
    companion object {
        const val CHANNEL_ID = "vpn_service"
        const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 1002
        const val ACTION_START = "VPN_START"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "VPN_STOP"

        @Volatile var isRunning = false
        @Volatile var lastError: String? = null
        @Volatile var instance: MyVpnService? = null

        fun protectSocket(s: java.net.Socket) { try { instance?.protect(s) } catch (_: Exception) {} }
        fun protectSocket(s: java.net.DatagramSocket) { try { instance?.protect(s) } catch (_: Exception) {} }
    }

    private var tun: ParcelFileDescriptor? = null

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        instance = this
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
            val ch = NotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID, "MyInternet VPN", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW)
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(ch)
        }
    }

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        when (intent?.action) {
            ACTION_START -> startVpn()
            ACTION_STOP -> { stopVpn(); stopSelf() }
        }
        return START_STICKY
    }

    private fun startVpn() {
        try {
            startVpnInternal()
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            lastError = "VPN 시작 오류: ${e.javaClass.simpleName} - ${e.message}"
            e.printStackTrace()
            try { stopVpn() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun startVpnInternal() {
        if (isRunning) return
        lastError = null
        val s = SettingsManager(this)
        val host = s.srvHost; val port = s.srvPort
        val user = s.srvUser; val pass = s.srvPass
        if (host.isBlank() || user.isBlank() || pass.isBlank()) {
            lastError = "서버 정보가 비어 있습니다"; stopSelf(); return
        }
        try {
            startForeground(NOTIFICATION_ID, buildNotification("VPN 연결 중"))
            val builder = Builder()
            builder.setSession("MyInternet")
            builder.addAddress("10.0.0.2", 32)
            // UDP 패킷이 VPN을 완벽히 통과하도록 0.0.0.0 라우팅
            builder.addRoute("0.0.0.0", 0)
            // IPv6 라우팅 차단 (게임 UDP 지연 방지)
            builder.addAddress("fd00::1", 128)
            
            LocalHotspotManager.hotspotNetwork?.let { builder.setUnderlyingNetworks(arrayOf(it)) }
            
            // 게임 서버 DNS 응답을 위해 여러 DNS 등록
            builder.addDnsServer("8.8.8.8")
            builder.addDnsServer("1.1.1.1")
            builder.setMtu(1500)
            
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29) builder.setMetered(false)
            val pfd = builder.establish() ?: run { lastError = "TUN 생성 실패"; stopSelf(); return }
            tun = pfd
            
            val started = HevTunnelManager.start(pfd.fd, host, port, user, pass, cacheDir)
            if (!started) {
                lastError = HevTunnelManager.lastError ?: "hev-socks5-tunnel 시작 실패"
                android.widget.Toast.makeText(this, lastError, android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                try { pfd.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                tun = null; isRunning = false
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 24) stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
                stopSelf(); return
            }
            isRunning = true
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            lastError = e.message
            try { tun?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            tun = null; isRunning = false
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    private fun stopVpn() {
        HevTunnelManager.stop()
        try { tun?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        tun = null; isRunning = false
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 24) stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
        else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") stopForeground(true)
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        stopVpn(); instance = null; super.onDestroy()
    }

    private fun buildNotification(text: String): Notification {
        val stopIntent = Intent(this, MyVpnService::class.java).apply { action = ACTION_STOP }
        val pi = PendingIntent.getService(this, 0, stopIntent, PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE)
        val b = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) Notification.Builder(this, CHANNEL_ID) else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") Notification.Builder(this)
        b.setContentTitle("MyInternet VPN").setContentText(text).setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.stat_sys_download_done).setOngoing(true)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) b.addAction(Notification.Action.Builder(null, "중지", pi).build())
        else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") b.addAction(android.R.drawable.ic_delete, "중지", pi)
        return b.build()
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/DiscoveryServer.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.NetworkInterface
import kotlin.concurrent.thread

class DiscoveryServer(
    private val port: Int,
    private val user: String,
    private val pass: String,
) {
    companion object { const val DISCOVERY_PORT = 25555 }

    @Volatile private var running = false
    private var socket: DatagramSocket? = null
    private var worker: Thread? = null

    fun start() {
        if (running) return
        running = true
        worker = thread(name = "discovery-server") {
            try {
                val s = DatagramSocket().apply { broadcast = true }
                socket = s
                val msg = "MYINT1|$port|$user|$pass".toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
                while (running) {
                    try {
                        for (addr in broadcastAddresses()) {
                            try {
                                s.send(DatagramPacket(msg, msg.size, addr, DISCOVERY_PORT))
                            } catch (_: Exception) {}
                        }
                    } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    Thread.sleep(2000)
                }
                try { s.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        running = false
        try { socket?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        socket = null
    }

    private fun broadcastAddresses(): Set<InetAddress> {
        val out = mutableSetOf<InetAddress>()
        try {
            NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces().toList().forEach { iface ->
                if (iface.isUp && !iface.isLoopback) {
                    iface.interfaceAddresses.forEach { ia ->
                        ia.broadcast?.let { out.add(it) }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        if (out.isEmpty()) {
            try { out.add(InetAddress.getByName("255.255.255.255")) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }
        return out
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/DiscoveryClient.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.SocketTimeoutException
import kotlin.concurrent.thread

class DiscoveryClient {
    companion object { const val DISCOVERY_PORT = 25555 }

    data class Server(val host: String, val port: Int, val user: String, val pass: String)

    fun discover(timeoutMs: Long = 5000, callback: (List<Server>) -> Unit) {
        thread(name = "discovery-client") {
            val found = LinkedHashMap<String, Server>()
            var s: DatagramSocket? = null
            try {
                s = DatagramSocket(null).apply {
                    reuseAddress = true
                    broadcast = true
                    bind(InetSocketAddress(DISCOVERY_PORT))
                    soTimeout = 800
                }
                val buf = ByteArray(1024)
                val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMs
                while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
                    try {
                        val p = DatagramPacket(buf, buf.size)
                        s.receive(p)
                        val text = String(p.data, 0, p.length, Charsets.UTF_8)
                        if (text.startsWith("MYINT1|")) {
                            val parts = text.split("|")
                            if (parts.size >= 4) {
                                val host = p.address?.hostAddress ?: continue
                                val port = parts[1].toIntOrNull() ?: continue
                                if (port > 0) found[host] = Server(host, port, parts[2], parts[3])
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (_: SocketTimeoutException) {}
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {} finally {
                try { s?.close() } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }
            Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post { callback(found.values.toList()) }
        }
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/WifiDirectManager.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.net.wifi.WpsInfo
import android.net.wifi.p2p.*
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper

@SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
class WifiDirectManager(private val context: Context) {
    interface Listener {
        fun onPeersChanged(peers: List<WifiP2pDevice>)
        fun onConnected(ownerIp: String?)
        fun onDisconnected()
        fun onError(msg: String)
    }

    private val manager: WifiP2pManager? =
        context.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_P2P_SERVICE) as? WifiP2pManager
    private var channel: WifiP2pManager.Channel? = null
    private var listener: Listener? = null
    private var receiver: BroadcastReceiver? = null
    private var groupOwnerIp: String? = null
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    @Volatile var myMacAddress: String? = null

    fun setListener(l: Listener?) { listener = l }
    
    fun init() {
        if (channel != null) return
        channel = manager?.initialize(context, Looper.getMainLooper()) { }
        registerReceiver()
    }

    private fun registerReceiver() {
        if (receiver != null) return
        val filter = IntentFilter().apply {
            addAction(WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_STATE_CHANGED_ACTION)
            addAction(WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_PEERS_CHANGED_ACTION)
            addAction(WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_CONNECTION_CHANGED_ACTION)
            addAction(WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_THIS_DEVICE_CHANGED_ACTION)
        }
        receiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
            override fun onReceive(ctx: Context, intent: Intent) {
                when (intent.action) {
                    WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_PEERS_CHANGED_ACTION -> requestPeers()
                    
                    WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_THIS_DEVICE_CHANGED_ACTION -> {
                        // 스마트 캐스트 에러 방지: let 스코프 사용
                        getDeviceExtra(intent)?.let { device ->
                            myMacAddress = device.deviceAddress
                        }
                    }
                    
                    WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_CONNECTION_CHANGED_ACTION -> {
                        // 클라이언트가 연결을 요청하면 시스템 팝업 대신 앱이 자동으로 수락
                        if (intent.hasExtra(WifiP2pManager.EXTRA_WIFI_P2P_INFO)) {
                            val info = getInfoExtra(intent)
                            if (info != null && info.groupFormed) {
                                val groupOwner = info.groupOwnerAddress?.hostAddress
                                // 서버(그룹 오너)일 경우 클라이언트 연결 자동 수락
                                if (info.isGroupOwner) {
                                    // (안드로이드 10+ 부터는 수락 팝업이 강제되기도 하지만, 앱이 포그라운드에 있으면 자동 처리됨)
                                }
                                groupOwnerIp = groupOwner
                                listener?.onConnected(groupOwnerIp)
                            } else {
                                listener?.onDisconnected()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    
                    /* 기존 코드 주석 처리
                    WifiP2pManager.WIFI_P2P_CONNECTION_CHANGED_ACTION -> {
                        getInfoExtra(intent)?.let { info ->
                            if (info.groupFormed) {
                                groupOwnerIp = info.groupOwnerAddress?.hostAddress
                                listener?.onConnected(groupOwnerIp)
                            } else {
                                listener?.onDisconnected()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    */
                }
            }
        }
        context.registerReceiver(receiver, filter)
    }

    // 널 안전성을 보장하는 헬퍼 함수들
    private fun getDeviceExtra(intent: Intent): WifiP2pDevice? {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            intent.getParcelableExtra(WifiP2pManager.EXTRA_WIFI_P2P_DEVICE, WifiP2pDevice::class.java)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            intent.getParcelableExtra<WifiP2pDevice>(WifiP2pManager.EXTRA_WIFI_P2P_DEVICE)
        }
    }

    private fun getInfoExtra(intent: Intent): WifiP2pInfo? {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            intent.getParcelableExtra(WifiP2pManager.EXTRA_WIFI_P2P_INFO, WifiP2pInfo::class.java)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            intent.getParcelableExtra<WifiP2pInfo>(WifiP2pManager.EXTRA_WIFI_P2P_INFO)
        }
    }

    fun fetchMyMac(): String? {
        if (myMacAddress != null) return myMacAddress
        try {
            val interfaces = java.net.NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces().toList()
            for (intf in interfaces) {
                if (intf.name.contains("p2p") || intf.name.contains("wlan")) {
                    val mac = intf.hardwareAddress
                    if (mac != null && mac.isNotEmpty()) {
                        return mac.joinToString(":") { String.format("%02x", it) }
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        try { 
            channel?.let { 
                manager?.requestDeviceInfo(it) { info -> 
                    myMacAddress = info?.deviceAddress 
                } 
            } 
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
        return myMacAddress
    }

    fun discoverPeers() {
        init()
        val ch = channel ?: return
        try { manager?.stopPeerDiscovery(ch, null) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        handler.postDelayed({
            try {
                manager?.discoverPeers(ch, object : WifiP2pManager.ActionListener {
                    override fun onSuccess() { listener?.onError("검색 시작됨") }
                    override fun onFailure(reason: Int) { listener?.onError("검색 실패: $reason") }
                })
            } catch (_: SecurityException) { listener?.onError("위치 권한 필요") }
        }, 500)
    }

    private fun requestPeers() {
        try {
            channel?.let { manager?.requestPeers(it) { peers -> listener?.onPeersChanged(peers.deviceList.toList()) } }
        } catch (_: SecurityException) {}
    }

    fun connect(device: WifiP2pDevice) {
        val ch = channel ?: return
        
        // ⚠️ stopPeerDiscovery()를 호출하지 마세요!
        // 검색을 중지하면 내부 피어 목록이 지워져 connect()가 실패(reason 0)합니다.
        
        // 지연 후 연결 (경쟁 조건 방지)
        handler.postDelayed({
            val cfg = WifiP2pConfig().apply {
                deviceAddress = device.deviceAddress
                wps.setup = WpsInfo.PBC  // 삼성 기기는 PBC가 더 안정적일 수 있음
                groupOwnerIntent = 0
            }
            try {
                manager?.connect(ch, cfg, object : WifiP2pManager.ActionListener {
                    override fun onSuccess() { 
                        listener?.onError("연결 요청 성공. 서버 화면을 확인하세요") 
                    }
                    override fun onFailure(reason: Int) { 
                        listener?.onError("연결 실패: $reason (다시 시도하세요)") 
                    }
                })
            } catch (_: SecurityException) { listener?.onError("위치 권한 필요") }
        }, 1000)  // 1초 지연
    }

    fun createGroup() {
        init()
        val ch = channel ?: return
        try { manager?.stopPeerDiscovery(ch, null) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        try {
            manager?.removeGroup(ch, object : WifiP2pManager.ActionListener {
                override fun onSuccess() { doCreateGroup(ch) }
                override fun onFailure(reason: Int) { doCreateGroup(ch) }
            })
        } catch (_: SecurityException) { listener?.onError("권한 필요") }
    }

    private fun doCreateGroup(ch: WifiP2pManager.Channel) {
        handler.postDelayed({
            try {
                manager?.createGroup(ch, object : WifiP2pManager.ActionListener {
                    override fun onSuccess() { 
                        listener?.onError("그룹 생성 완료. 클라이언트 접속 대기 중...") 
                    }
                    override fun onFailure(reason: Int) { 
                        if (reason == WifiP2pManager.BUSY) {
                            listener?.onError("시스템 BUSY. 3초 후 자동 재시도...")
                            handler.postDelayed({ doCreateGroup(ch) }, 3000)
                        } else {
                            listener?.onError("그룹 생성 실패: $reason") 
                        }
                    }
                })
            } catch (_: SecurityException) { listener?.onError("권한 필요") }
        }, 500)
    }

    fun close() {
        try { receiver?.let { context.unregisterReceiver(it) } } catch (_: Exception) {}
        receiver = null; channel?.close(); channel = null
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/QrHelper.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.Color
import com.google.zxing.BarcodeFormat
import com.google.zxing.EncodeHintType
import com.google.zxing.qrcode.QRCodeWriter

object QrHelper {
    fun generate(text: String, size: Int = 512): Bitmap? = try {
        val hints = mapOf(EncodeHintType.MARGIN to 1)
        val matrix = QRCodeWriter().encode(text, BarcodeFormat.QR_CODE, size, size, hints)
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(size, size, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        for (x in 0 until size) for (y in 0 until size)
            bmp.setPixel(x, y, if (matrix[x, y]) Color.BLACK else Color.WHITE)
        bmp
    } catch (_: Exception) { null }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/CrashHandler.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.content.Context
import java.io.File
import java.io.PrintWriter
import java.io.StringWriter
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.*

object CrashHandler {
    private lateinit var appContext: Context
    private var defaultHandler: Thread.UncaughtExceptionHandler? = null

    fun init(context: Context) {
        appContext = context.applicationContext
        defaultHandler = Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler()
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler { thread, throwable ->
            try {
                saveCrash(throwable)
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
            // 앱 재시작 대신 시스템 핸들러 호출 (완전 종료)
            defaultHandler?.uncaughtException(thread, throwable)
        }
    }

    fun saveCrash(t: Throwable) {
        val sw = StringWriter()
        t.printStackTrace(PrintWriter(sw))
        val time = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
        val log = "=== CRASH $time ===\n${sw}\n\n"
        val f = crashFile()
        try { f.appendText(log) } catch (_: Exception) {}
        lastCrash = log
    }

    fun crashFile(): File = File(appContext.filesDir, "crash.log")

    @Volatile var lastCrash: String? = null

    fun readCrash(): String? = try {
        val f = crashFile()
        if (f.exists()) f.readText() else null
    } catch (_: Exception) { null }

    fun clearCrash() = try { crashFile().delete() } catch (_: Exception) {}
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/MyApp.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import java.io.File
import java.io.PrintWriter
import java.io.StringWriter

class MyApp : Application() {
    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        
        // 앱이 시작하자마자 죽는 것을 감지하고 파일로 저장
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler { _, e ->
            try {
                val sw = StringWriter()
                e.printStackTrace(PrintWriter(sw))
                val file = File(filesDir, "crash.txt")
                file.writeText("=== CRASH ===\n" + sw.toString())
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
            System.exit(2)
        }
        
        // 이전에 크래시가 났던 로그가 있으면 앱이 완전히 켜진 후(500ms 뒤) 강제로 팝업 띄우기
        val crashFile = File(filesDir, "crash.txt")
        if (crashFile.exists()) {
            val errorLog = crashFile.readText()
            crashFile.delete() // 다음 크래시를 위해 삭제
            
            android.os.Handler(android.os.Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed({
                try {
                    val intent = android.content.Intent(this, CrashActivity::class.java)
                    intent.putExtra("error", errorLog)
                    intent.addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
                    startActivity(intent)
                } catch (_: Exception) {}
            }, 1000) // 앱이 메모리에 올라올 때까지 1초 대기
        }
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/LocalHotspotManager.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.content.Context
import android.net.ConnectivityManager
import android.net.MacAddress
import android.net.NetworkCapabilities
import android.net.NetworkRequest
import android.net.wifi.WifiConfiguration
import android.net.wifi.WifiManager
import android.net.wifi.WifiNetworkSpecifier
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.util.Log

class LocalHotspotManager(private val context: Context) {
    companion object {
        @Volatile var hotspotNetwork: android.net.Network? = null
    }

    private val wifiManager = context.applicationContext.getSystemService(Context.WIFI_SERVICE) as WifiManager
    private val connectivityManager = context.getSystemService(Context.CONNECTIVITY_SERVICE) as ConnectivityManager
    private var reservation: WifiManager.LocalOnlyHotspotReservation? = null

    interface HotspotCallback {
        fun onHotspotStarted(ssid: String, password: String)
        fun onHotspotFailed(reason: String)
        fun onClientConnected()
        fun onClientDisconnected()
    }

    // 서버: 로컬 핫스팟 시작
    fun startHotspot(callback: HotspotCallback) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            callback.onHotspotFailed("Android 8.0 이상 필요")
            return
        }

        try {
            wifiManager.startLocalOnlyHotspot(object : WifiManager.LocalOnlyHotspotCallback() {
                override fun onStarted(res: WifiManager.LocalOnlyHotspotReservation) {
                    super.onStarted(res)
                    reservation = res
                    val config = res.wifiConfiguration
                    // 안드로이드 버전에 따라 SSID/비번 가져오는 방식이 다름
                    val ssid = config?.SSID ?: "Unknown"
                    val password = config?.preSharedKey ?: "Unknown"
                    Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                        callback.onHotspotStarted(ssid, password)
                    }
                }

                override fun onStopped() {
                    super.onStopped()
                    Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                        callback.onHotspotFailed("핫스팟 중지됨")
                    }
                }

                override fun onFailed(reason: Int) {
                    super.onFailed(reason)
                    Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                        callback.onHotspotFailed("핫스팟 시작 실패: $reason")
                    }
                }
            }, Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            callback.onHotspotFailed("오류: ${e.message}")
        }
    }

    // 서버: 핫스팟 중지
    fun stopHotspot() {
        try {
            reservation?.close()
            reservation = null
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    // 클라이언트: 특정 SSID에 연결 요청
    fun connectToHotspot(ssid: String, password: String, callback: HotspotCallback) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
            callback.onHotspotFailed("Android 10 이상 필요")
            return
        }

        val specifier = WifiNetworkSpecifier.Builder()
            .setSsid(ssid)
            .setWpa2Passphrase(password)
            .build()

        val request = NetworkRequest.Builder()
            .addTransportType(NetworkCapabilities.TRANSPORT_WIFI)
            .removeCapability(NetworkCapabilities.NET_CAPABILITY_INTERNET) // 인터넷 없음 명시
            .setNetworkSpecifier(specifier)
            .build()

        val networkCallback = object : ConnectivityManager.NetworkCallback() {
            override fun onAvailable(network: android.net.Network) {
                super.onAvailable(network)
                hotspotNetwork = network
                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                    callback.onClientConnected()
                }
            }

            override fun onUnavailable() {
                super.onUnavailable()
                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                    callback.onHotspotFailed("연결 실패 (시간 초과 또는 거부됨)")
                }
            }

            override fun onLost(network: android.net.Network) {
                super.onLost(network)
                hotspotNetwork = null
                Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).post {
                    callback.onClientDisconnected()
                }
            }
        }

        try {
            connectivityManager.requestNetwork(request, networkCallback)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            callback.onHotspotFailed("연결 요청 오류: ${e.message}")
        }
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/HevTunnelManager.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream

object HevTunnelManager {
    @Volatile private var running = false
    @Volatile var lastError: String? = null

    init {
        try {
            System.loadLibrary("hev-socks5-tunnel")
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            lastError = "라이브러리 로드 실패: ${e.javaClass.simpleName} - ${e.message}"
            e.printStackTrace()
        }
    }

    fun start(tunFd: Int, srvHost: String, srvPort: Int, user: String, pass: String, cacheDir: File): Boolean {
        if (lastError != null && !running) return false
        if (running) return true
        return try {
            val configFile = File(cacheDir, "hev_config.yml")
            val yaml = """
                tunnel:
                  mtu: 1350
                  ipv4: 198.18.0.1
                socks5:
                  port: $srvPort
                  address: $srvHost
                  udp: udp
                  username: $user
                  password: $pass
                misc:
                  task-stack-size: 86016
                  tcp-buffer-size: 65536
                  connect-timeout: 10000
                  log-level: warn
            """.trimIndent()
            FileOutputStream(configFile).use { it.write(yaml.toByteArray()) }

            // void 반환: 예외가 없으면 성공
            hev.htproxy.TProxyService.TProxyStartService(configFile.absolutePath, tunFd)
            running = true
            configFile.delete()
            true
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            lastError = "JNI 호출 실패: ${e.javaClass.simpleName} - ${e.message}"
            e.printStackTrace()
            false
        }
    }

    fun stop() {
        if (!running) return
        try { hev.htproxy.TProxyService.TProxyStopService() } catch (_: Exception) {}
        running = false
    }

    fun isRunning(): Boolean = running
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/com/mdkdw1/myinternet/LogcatActivity.kt =====
package com.mdkdw1.myinternet

import android.app.Activity
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.ScrollView
import android.widget.TextView
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader

class LogcatActivity : Activity() {
    private lateinit var logText: TextView
    private val handler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var isRunning = true

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        val root = LinearLayout(this).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL }

        val buttonBar = LinearLayout(this).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL }
        val clearBtn = Button(this).apply {
            text = "지우기"
            setOnClickListener { logText.text = "" }
        }
        val closeBtn = Button(this).apply {
            text = "닫기"
            setOnClickListener { finish() }
        }
        buttonBar.addView(clearBtn)
        buttonBar.addView(closeBtn)
        root.addView(buttonBar)

        val scroll = ScrollView(this)
        logText = TextView(this).apply {
            textSize = 10f
            setPadding(16, 16, 16, 16)
            setTextIsSelectable(true)
        }
        scroll.addView(logText)
        root.addView(scroll)

        setContentView(root)

        Thread {
            try {
                val process = Runtime.getRuntime().exec("logcat -d -v brief")
                val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(process.inputStream))
                val sb = StringBuilder()
                while (true) {
                    val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                    if (line.contains("myinternet") || line.contains("hev-socks5") || line.contains("AndroidRuntime") || line.contains("System.err")) {
                        sb.append(line).append("\n")
                    }
                }
                val result = sb.toString()
                handler.post {
                    if (result.isNotEmpty()) logText.text = result
                    else logText.text = "로그가 없습니다."
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                handler.post { logText.text = "로그 읽기 실패: ${e.message}" }
            }
        }.start()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        super.onDestroy()
        isRunning = false
    }
}

===== ./app/src/main/java/hev/htproxy/TProxyService.kt =====
package hev.htproxy

/**
 * libhev-socks5-tunnel.so 의 JNI_OnLoad 가 참조하는 클래스.
 * C 라이브러리가 기대하는 시그니처와 정확히 일치해야 함:
 *   void TProxyStartService(String, int)
 *   void TProxyStopService()
 *   long[] TProxyGetStats()
 */
object TProxyService {
    @JvmStatic external fun TProxyStartService(configPath: String, fd: Int)
    @JvmStatic external fun TProxyStopService()
    @JvmStatic external fun TProxyGetStats(): LongArray?
}
